# Specification: Schema versioning

## 1. Business Goal

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-14 (pin command name and placement) -->

A repository's `.spec/schema/` is a copy of the consumer's from one day, and nothing says which day, so either every repository moves with a consumer repository at once or the copies diverge silently, and a repository that lags cannot catch up on purpose; and a bare version number cannot tell a breaking schema change from a harmless one, so every change forces every consumer into a deliberate upgrade and the owner cannot try one on this repository alone. This Feature removes that failure state: the manifest pins one `major.minor.patch` `schemaVersion`, numbered by whether a tree's verdict can change; the tool embeds the versions it ships - every published one from `1.0.0` on - and checks with exactly the pinned one; `specht schema pin <version>` moves the pin up or down, and `specht upgrade` brings the schema set and the templates to the pin and prints what changed. Lagging becomes deliberate, moving becomes one command each way, the number says whether a move can fail the build, and a document is only ever changed by the agent, from the report.

## 2. User Needs

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-11 (need 9 and A-4 added, decision 0009) -->

| #   | Persona                                             | Need                                                                                                 | Pain Point Today                                                                                                                                          |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | The maintainer of a lagging repository              | Pin a version and stay there until ready; the newest tool still checks it                            | A fork from one day, with no version to name                                                                                                              |
| 2   | The maintainer moving a repository                  | One command that brings the schema set and templates to the pinned version and says what changed     | Hand-copy, then diff by eye                                                                                                                               |
| 3   | The agent migrating documents                       | After an upgrade, the check reports what each document is missing, with what the rule expected       | Nothing says what changed between versions                                                                                                                |
| 4   | A maintainer trying a schema edit locally           | Point the check at the on-disk schema files instead of the embedded ones, deliberately               | The on-disk copy is the only copy, so a hand edit is silently authoritative                                                                               |
| 5   | `0001-F4`                                           | The embedded version set and the newest version number to write                                      | N/A (internal dependency)                                                                                                                                 |
| 6   | `0001-F3`                                           | The version and the source to name in the report                                                     | N/A (internal dependency)                                                                                                                                 |
| 7   | The owner trying a schema change on this repository | A version number that says whether a change can fail a tree, and a pin that moves up or down         | An integer version cannot tell a breaking change from a non-breaking one, so every change forces every consumer into a deliberate upgrade (decision 0005) |
| 8   | A maintainer with a template of its own             | Keep that template across `upgrade`, and still get the pinned version's copy of every other template | `upgrade` rewrites every template with the pinned version's; the frontmatter schemas have an opt-out (B-029) and the templates have none (decision 0006)  |
| 9   | The owner changing a shipped file                   | Know which version is still open to change, and start the next one with one command                  | The folders are `v1` and state no version, nothing marks one published, and a new one is a hand copy (decision 0009)                                      |

### Assumptions

| ID  | Assumption                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A-1 | A manifest with no `schemaVersion` key is `0.1.0`, the version that predates the key. the baseline manifest at the baseline commit has no key and must keep checking unchanged (Should-7). Amended 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): was version 1.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| A-2 | `0.1.0` is the schema set the first published package embeds (decision 0002, decision 0005): `0001-F1` A-3's set, plus the manifest keys `0001-F5` and `0001-F6` add, plus the optional `title` and `description` keys decision 0003 adds to the epic frontmatter schema, plus `0001-F5` decision 0004's loosening of a Feature's `epic` and a task's `parent` and `id`, since nothing is published before brief § 8 step 5. Amended 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): was version 1. Amended 2026-10-11 (decision 0007): the first publish no longer waits for brief § 8 step 5, so `0.1.0` is the set as it stands when the first tag is pushed; which of the parts listed here it then holds is OQ-30. |
| A-3 | "Prints what changed" is the name of every file rewritten and the pinned version, and no diff (OQ-3, resolved 2026-10-08). Provisional on OQ-21: decision 0005 removed the move the owner's version pair described, and whether `upgrade` also names the version the files were at is not decided.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| A-4 | The version lock is one committed file in this repository that records each locked schema version with one digest (decision 0009). A version it records is locked; the one it does not is the working version, open to change. A version is locked before it is published (`0055-F6` B-013), so every published version is locked. Its path and format are § 7's (OQ-52).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## 4. Constraints

<!-- last written by: spec-author, 2026-10-24 (C-8, C-9, C-10, C-14 and C-15 amended, decision 0016) -->

| ID   | Constraint                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Rules Out                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| C-1  | A schema version, once published in a package, is never changed in place; from `1.0.0` on it is embedded in every later tool and never deleted, and a later tool may stop embedding a published `0.x.y` (decision 0002; amended 2026-10-09, decision 0005; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007, resolving OQ-10: was every published version embedded for good, with `0.x` open).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Patching a published version in place, a `0.x.y` included; dropping a published `1.0.0` or higher "nobody uses it"; counting an unpublished step-3 pack as shipped; a freeze that starts when the `specht.tool` package reaches `1.0.0` (C-12).                                                |
| C-2  | `upgrade` writes only `<root>/.specht/manifest.json` and under `<root>/.specht/schema/frontmatter/` and `<root>/.specht/templates/` (amended 2026-10-11, `0001-F5` decision 0011: was under `<root>/.specht/schema/` and the templates folder), and with the on-disk source selected and no upstream source recorded it writes no frontmatter schema (B-029; amended 2026-10-08, OQ-8); it writes no template the manifest names as the consumer's, and touches no file under that templates folder the pinned version does not ship (B-060, B-063; amended 2026-10-09, decision 0006).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Rewriting a specification, an epic, a record or a `.feature`; `--fix`; overwriting a consumer's own frontmatter schemas; overwriting a template the consumer has named as its own; deleting or tidying a template file the tool never shipped.                                                 |
| C-3  | Retired 2026-10-09 (decision 0005): `upgrade` moves one version at a time.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Nothing: the owner dropped it. The pin command moves to any shipped version (B-043, B-044), and `upgrade` brings the files to the pin (B-005).                                                                                                                                                 |
| C-4  | The consumer's manifest is always read from `<root>/.specht/manifest.json`; the pin selects the frontmatter schemas, rule vocabulary and manifest interpretation, and omitted settings are filled from that version's shipped defaults without replacing explicit consumer settings (`0001-F5` B-048, B-049; owner, 2026-10-13; `0001-F5` decision 0010). Only the frontmatter schemas have a selectable source.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | An embedded manifest replacing a missing consumer file; defaults winning over explicit sections, grammars, roles or rule settings; newest-version interpretation applied to an older pin; a schema-source switch also switching the manifest.                                                  |
| C-5  | The tool, not the consumer, knows the difference between two versions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | A migration script shipped to the consumer; a consumer-side changelog the tool reads.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| C-6  | The `$id` of each frontmatter schema in specht's own embedded set is `https://github.com/rlittlesii/specht/schema/`, then its version segment, `v<major.minor.patch>`, the folder's name (B-086, B-087), then `/frontmatter/` and its file name, as the file sits under `.specht/` (amended 2026-10-11, `0001-F5` decision 0011; the mirrored path is derived by spec-author, not stated by the owner: a default spec-author proposed, contained in a plan the owner approved on 2026-10-11); a consumer's upstream schema carries its own `$id` (decision 0004; amended 2026-10-08, OQ-7). Amended 2026-10-11 (decision 0009, resolving OQ-22): the segment's form was provisional, the integer `v<n>`.                                                                                                                           | A version-less `$id`; an integer or ordinal segment; a bare `0.1.0` segment with no `v`; an `$id` under another repository for specht's set; a consumer's schema forced under `rlittlesii/specht`; an `$id` whose path past the version differs from the file's path under `.specht/schema/`.  |
| C-7  | Every path `upgrade` derives and prints is relative to the root with `/` separators; a path the user typed (`--root`) is echoed as given.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | An absolute path for a rewritten file; a `--root` argument rewritten before it is echoed.                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| C-8  | `init` and `upgrade` are the only commands that may make a network call, and only to fetch an upstream schema source the manifest records or the same init run will record - its frontmatter schemas and the templates it hashes (0001-F4 decision 0003, ratified 2026-10-12; amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016: was its frontmatter schemas only); the check never makes one (decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | A fetch, a cached remote read or a version lookup at check time; a network call without a recorded source or a source the same init run will record; a template fetched without a hash recorded for it.                                                                                        |
| C-9  | An upstream schema source is recorded with its version and one content hash for each fetched file, a template included, and a fetched file is written only when its content matches its hash (decision 0004). Amended 2026-10-11 (owner; decision 0008, ratification): was a content hash. Amended 2026-10-24 (decision 0016): a template the record hashes is a fetched file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | An unpinned or "latest" source; content written before its hash is checked; one hash over the whole set; one hash over the templates.                                                                                                                                                          |
| C-10 | An upstream schema source's version and content hashes are independent of `schemaVersion`: `schemaVersion` pins the rule vocabulary and the templates the manifest neither names as the consumer's (amended 2026-10-09, decision 0006) nor the record hashes, and the recorded upstream version pins the frontmatter schemas and the templates the record hashes (amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016: was the frontmatter schemas only); only the consumer moves the upstream version, by editing the manifest (owner, 2026-10-08; decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `upgrade` moving the upstream version; an upstream version derived from `schemaVersion`, or the reverse; `upgrade` rewriting a template the record hashes with the pinned version's.                                                                                                           |
| C-11 | A schema version is numbered by its verdict: a patch changes no tree's verdict; from `1.0.0` on a minor only accepts more, so no tree that passed fails, and any change under which a tree that passed can fail - a new `SPEC###` included - is a major; before `1.0.0` a `0.minor` step may fail a tree that passed, a new `SPEC###` included (decision 0005; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007: was one rule for every version).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | From `1.0.0` on, a new rule or a stricter schema in a minor; at any version, a new rule or a stricter schema in a patch, and a patch that changes any verdict; numbering by the size of the change, by date or by tool release.                                                                |
| C-12 | A schema version is numbered independently of the `specht.tool` package version; neither is derived from the other (decision 0005).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Reading the schema version from `version.json` or the assembly; a tool release that must bump the schema version; a schema version that must bump the tool's version.                                                                                                                          |
| C-13 | Once a manifest exists, its `schemaVersion` changes only through `specht schema pin` or a hand edit, and `specht schema pin` writes nothing else (decision 0005).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | A pin that also rewrites the schemas or the templates; an `upgrade` that moves the pin; `init` rewriting an existing manifest's pin.                                                                                                                                                           |
| C-14 | A template is the consumer's only because the manifest names it (derived from OQ-25 and OQ-8, not stated by the owner; decision 0006), and a recorded upstream source neither makes a template the consumer's nor writes one the manifest names (owner, 2026-10-24; amended 2026-10-24, decision 0016, resolving OQ-26: was a reading that did not prejudge the upstream template source).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Ownership inferred from the on-disk schema source, from a hand edit or a difference in content, from the file being one the tool does not ship, or from a hash the record holds; an all-or-nothing switch over the templates folder; an upstream template written over one the manifest names. |
| C-15 | The first release fetches from a consumer's own upstream source on any `https` host without authentication: the tool reads no credential and sends none with a fetch, and an authenticated source is the next release's (owner, 2026-10-24: "Private sources should be allowed in the first release." and "Assume the private source has no authentication for this version. We can do authenticated sources NEXT version."; decision 0016, resolving OQ-41). Amended 2026-10-24 (decision 0016): was public sources only (owner, 2026-10-11: "public sources for v1. for v2 we should offer pats in the keychain or os equivalent"; decision 0008, ratification), with "the first release" for "v1" spec-author's reading.                                                                                                        | A token read from the environment, `SPECHT_SCHEMA_TOKEN` included; a token in the manifest or in an argument; a read of a credential store; answering a credential challenge; refusing a source because its host is not specht's.                                                              |
| C-16 | The name of a version's folder is the one statement of which version its files are: the same name in every tree a version's files sit in (B-086) and in the embedded resource names (owner, 2026-10-11; decision 0009).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | A mapping from a folder to its version kept in code; a folder named by an integer or an ordinal; trees that name one version differently; ordering versions by folder name as text, so `v0.10.0` before `v0.9.0`.                                                                              |
| C-17 | The version lock records one digest for each locked version and no list of its files, and a digest it records is never rewritten once its version is published: a changed file under a published version is a new version. A locked version no published package has carried may be relocked, and schema `0.2.0` is relocked when the nine rules B-115 adds land in it (B-013; owner, 2026-10-11; decision 0009; one digest and no file list is the approved plan's; amended 2026-10-14, decision 0014, resolving OQ-54: was a digest never rewritten and a changed file under any locked version a new version). How a relock is made - the unpublished version's entry removed by hand in a reviewed change, then recorded again by the lock target (B-092) - is spec-author's reading, not stated by the owner (decision 0014). | Locking a published version again so a changed version passes; a published version's lock entry edited by hand; a lock target that rewrites an entry it finds (B-093); a file list in the lock, the index `0001-F3` § 5 row 10 and `0001-F10` C-2 keep out; a freeze held by review alone.     |

B-056.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Until it is answered every version the tool ships is embedded (C-1; amended 2026-10-11, decision 0007: was every version), and only an upstream source the manifest records is fetched (decision 0004).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Was: decided by the owner as a later stage (decision 0006), its shape open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

OQ-59.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
